Title: Un Ymadrodd ar bumtheg ynghylch Jesu Grist, a'n Prynedigaeth trwyddo Ef; neu, ynghylch Ail Erthygl y Credo: allan o Bregethau y gwir-barchedig Ordinari y Brodyr yn Berlin yn y Flwyddyn 1738; o gyfieithad Evan Williams; anghwanegir hefyd rai hymnau
Creator: Nikolaus Ludwig, Count von Zinzendorf (1700-1760), named on the title page as 'Ordinari y Brodyr'; translated by Evan Williams
Publisher: Llundain (London): argraphwyd yn y flwyddyn MDCCLX (printer not named in the scan)
Publication Date: 1760
Rights: Public Domain (published 1760, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: cym
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_un-ymadrodd-ar-bumtheg-y_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1760 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_un-ymadrodd-ar-bumtheg-y_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1760), the item's own bim_eighteenth-century_un-ymadrodd-ar-bumtheg-y_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1760_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1760. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 0c76cc8976dd608548d6714f4fdc86b9c5f4e426590a297008dc0726af56484c.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. British Library microfilm scan; the title page is legible, the discourses readable with the long s kept and some noise. The title page states that the translation was compared with the latest German edition.
Content note: The second series of Zinzendorf's Berlin discourses of 1738, on the second article of the Creed, in Welsh, with some hymns added; the Moravian message as Welsh hearers met it in the years when Harris's Trevecka family drew on Moravian models.
